{"parentUuid":"157b02c3-5aac-4251-aeda-a1327b97c1b2","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"58479281-f0fc-437a-b3c2-51154bb8cfd1","agentId":"a3d0211873f170d2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FLJPrY6tNY6x5bQw1TzxAg","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The spine's segment MANIFEST — the sole linearization point for the retain-all segment set.\n2\t\n3\tThe spine is stored as a sequence of size-bounded, immutable **sealed** segments plus exactly one\n4\t**active** (append) segment; the manifest names them in seq order. It is deliberately UNSIGNED and\n5\tNON-LOAD-BEARING: record order, count, and every tamper decision are re-derived from the segment BYTES\n6\t(via `verify_chain`/`classify_head`), never from the manifest's convenience fields. A doctored manifest\n7\ttherefore fails CLOSED — dropping/reordering an interior segment breaks `prev_hash`/seq contiguity, a\n8\tdropped tail shortens `entries()` (the signed head's `n < entry_count` gate fires), a dropped front fails\n9\tthe genesis check. (A future signed-manifest tier would set `manifest_sig` — reserved `null` — because it\n10\twould then attest pruned ranges; retain-all needs no such trust.)\n11\t\n12\t`SpineLayout` derives the whole on-disk layout from the spine data-file path a `SpineStore` is constructed\n13\twith, so a store on a temp path is fully isolated. This module does no locking — the caller (`SpineStore`)\n14\tholds the cross-process flock while reading/writing/migrating.\n15\t\"\"\"\n16\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n17\t\n18\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n19\tfrom pathlib import PurePosixPath, Path\n20\t\n21\tfrom pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator\n22\t\n23\tfrom ..reuse.chain import _GENESIS_PREV\n24\tfrom .atomicio import atomic_write_text\n25\t\n26\tSEGMENT_STEM = \"seg-\"\n27\t_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tdef segment_filename(seg_id: int, codec: str = \"none\") -> str:\n31\t    \"\"\"Zero-padded segment file name; `.gz` suffix when gzip-sealed (Slice 4).\"\"\"\n32\t    base = f\"{SEGMENT_STEM}{seg_id:08d}.jsonl\"\n33\t    return base + \".gz\" if codec == \"gzip\" else base\n34\t\n35\t\n36\tclass Segment(BaseModel):\n37\t    \"\"\"One segment in the manifest. `first_prev_hash`/`boundary_hash` are the chain-linkage the reader\n38\t    CROSS-CHECKS against the segment's real first/last records — they are convenience, not authority.\"\"\"\n39\t    model_config = ConfigDict(extra=\"ignore\")   # forward-compatible: tolerate fields a newer writer added\n40\t    id: int = Field(ge=0)\n41\t    file: str                                    # path RELATIVE to the spine dir, e.g. \"segments/seg-00000000.jsonl\"\n42\t    codec: str = \"none\"                          # \"none\" | \"gzip\"\n43\t    sealed: bool = False\n44\t    first_seq: int = Field(ge=0)\n45\t    last_seq: int | None = None                  # null while active\n46\t    count: int | None = None                     # null while active; CONVENIENCE ONLY — never a tamper input\n47\t    first_prev_hash: str = _GENESIS_PREV         # == the prior segment's boundary_hash (seg 0 == genesis)\n48\t    boundary_hash: str | None = None             # == entry_hash(last_seq); null while active\n49\t    bytes: int = 0\n50\t    sha256: str | None = None                    # at-rest file integrity; NOT a chain digest\n51\t\n52\t    @field_validator(\"file\")\n53\t    @classmethod\n54\t    def _file_is_contained_relative(cls, v: str) -> str:\n55\t        \"\"\"A segment `file` must be a plain relative path under the spine dir — never absolute and never\n56\t        `..`-escaping. `get()`/`iter_records()`/`tail()` open segment files WITHOUT running verify(), so a\n57\t        doctored manifest pointing `file` at `/etc/passwd` or `../../secret` would otherwise surface that\n58\t        file's bytes AS spine records. Reject it at the model boundary so the read path stays fail-closed\n59\t        even though the manifest itself is unsigned.\"\"\"\n60\t        pp = PurePosixPath(v)\n61\t        if not v or pp.is_absolute() or PurePosixPath(v.replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\")).is_absolute() or \"..\" in pp.parts:\n62\t            raise ValueError(f\"segment file must be a contained relative path, got {v!r}\")\n63\t        return v\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tclass Manifest(BaseModel):\n67\t    model_config = ConfigDict(extra=\"ignore\")\n68\t    schema_version: int = _SCHEMA_VERSION\n69\t    generation: int = Field(default=0, ge=0)     # monotonic epoch; ++ on EVERY swap — the unified change token\n70\t    scope: str = \"\"\n71\t    segments: list[Segment] = Field(default_factory=list)\n72\t    manifest_sig: str | None = None              # reserved for the deferred signed-manifest tier\n73\t\n74\t    def active(self) -> Segment | None:\n75\t        \"\"\"The single unsealed (append) segment, if any. There is at most one.\"\"\"\n76\t        act = [s for s in self.segments if not s.sealed]\n77\t        return act[-1] if act else None\n78\t\n79\t    def sealed_in_order(self) -> list[Segment]:\n80\t        return sorted((s for s in self.segments if s.sealed), key=lambda s: s.first_seq)\n81\t\n82\t    def ordered(self) -> list[Segment]:\n83\t        \"\"\"All segments in seq order (sealed by first_seq, then the active tail).\"\"\"\n84\t        out = self.sealed_in_order()\n85\t        act = self.active()\n86\t        if act is not None:\n87\t            out.append(act)\n88\t        return out\n89\t\n90\t\n91\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n92\tclass SpineLayout:\n93\t    \"\"\"The on-disk layout derived from a spine data-file path (…/spine.jsonl). Every artifact is\n94\t    NAMESPACED BY THE DATA FILE'S STEM (`spine.manifest.json`, `spine.lock`, `spine.segments/`) so two\n95\t    stores whose data files merely SHARE a directory — e.g. the `tempfile.mktemp()` idiom that drops files\n96\t    straight into /tmp — never collide on a manifest/lock/segments set. Without this, one migrated store's\n97\t    `manifest.json` would be read by every other store in that directory and silently steer it onto the\n98\t    wrong segment (a real cross-store contamination the review caught).\"\"\"\n99\t    data_path: Path            # the legacy/identity path (…/spine.jsonl) — the stable lock/identity token\n100\t    spine_dir: Path\n101\t    segments_dir: Path\n102\t    manifest_path: Path\n103\t    lockfile_path: Path\n104\t    trash_dir: Path\n105\t\n106\t    @classmethod\n107\t    def for_path(cls, data_path: Path | str) -> \"SpineLayout\":\n108\t        p = Path(data_path)\n109\t        d = p.parent\n110\t        ns = p.stem or p.name       # \"spine.jsonl\" -> \"spine\"; namespaces every derived artifact\n111\t        return cls(\n112\t            data_path=p, spine_dir=d, segments_dir=d / f\"{ns}.segments\",\n113\t            manifest_path=d / f\"{ns}.manifest.json\", lockfile_path=d / f\"{ns}.lock\", trash_dir=d / f\"{ns}.trash\",\n114\t        )\n115\t\n116\t    def seg_path(self, seg: Segment) -> Path:\n117\t        \"\"\"Absolute path of a segment's file (its `file` is stored relative to the spine dir). The\n118\t        `Segment.file` validator guarantees it is a plain relative path that cannot escape spine_dir.\"\"\"\n119\t        return self.spine_dir / seg.file\n120\t\n121\t    def segment_rel(self, seg_id: int, codec: str = \"none\") -> str:\n122\t        \"\"\"The manifest `file` value (relative to spine_dir) for a segment id — under the namespaced\n123\t        segments dir, e.g. 'spine.segments/seg-00000000.jsonl'.\"\"\"\n124\t        return f\"{self.segments_dir.name}/{segment_filename(seg_id, codec)}\"\n125\t\n126\t\n127\tdef read_manifest(layout: SpineLayout) -> Manifest | None:\n128\t    \"\"\"Load the manifest, or None if absent. A present-but-unparseable manifest RAISES (fail closed) —\n129\t    the atomic write path guarantees it is never torn, so a parse failure means genuine corruption, not a\n130\t    partial write, and must never be silently treated as 'no manifest' (which would strand the segments).\"\"\"\n131\t    mp = layout.manifest_path\n132\t    if not mp.exists():\n133\t        return None\n134\t    return Manifest.model_validate_json(mp.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n135\t\n136\t\n137\tdef write_manifest(layout: SpineLayout, manifest: Manifest) -> None:\n138\t    \"\"\"Atomically publish the manifest (temp→fsync→os.replace→dir-fsync). THE cutover commit instant.\"\"\"\n139\t    layout.manifest_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n140\t    atomic_write_text(layout.manifest_path, manifest.model_dump_json(), prefix=\".manifest-\")\n141\t\n142\t\n143\tdef initial_manifest(scope: str, *, active_file: str, first_seq: int = 0,\n144\t                     first_prev_hash: str = _GENESIS_PREV) -> Manifest:\n145\t    \"\"\"A single-active-segment manifest (generation 0) — the shape produced by a fresh store or a\n146\t    migration of an existing single-file spine.\"\"\"\n147\t    return Manifest(\n148\t        generation=0, scope=scope,\n149\t        segments=[Segment(id=0, file=active_file, codec=\"none\", sealed=False,\n150\t                          first_seq=first_seq, first_prev_hash=first_prev_hash)],\n151\t    )\n152\t"}]},"uuid":"70114010-d815-4836-926a-f3194a4aa159","timestamp":"2026-07-19T16:22:43.729Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"157b02c3-5aac-4251-aeda-a1327b97c1b2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"70114010-d815-4836-926a-f3194a4aa159","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a3d0211873f170d2c","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdBixV3VayboUKzGnsFtU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Sfx22N4qCVe12Tm4kBFQfc","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/governor/killswitch.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":12350,"cache_creation_input_tokens":19431,"cache_read_input_tokens":37360,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":19431,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdBixTsYeAdSX2Ag4zkrG","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"24358ec0-df9c-4cb4-90f8-48c8048def17","timestamp":"2026-07-19T16:22:44.197Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"24358ec0-df9c-4cb4-90f8-48c8048def17","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"58479281-f0fc-437a-b3c2-51154bb8cfd1","agentId":"a3d0211873f170d2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Sfx22N4qCVe12Tm4kBFQfc","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The kill switch (SIGIL §5) — a single latch that halts the agent mesh while leaving perception and\n2\tmemory-read (A0 observe) alive. State is append-only on the spine.\n3\t\n4\tASYMMETRIC AUTHENTICATION (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2): halting is always the safe direction, so ANY\n5\tengage event halts (a nuisance forged-engage is at worst a fail-safe DoS). UN-halting is the\n6\tdangerous direction, so a RELEASE is honored ONLY if it is signed by the owner key and verifies\n7\tagainst the trusted pubkey — a forged release can never revive a halted mesh. Read fresh per decision\n8\tso a release (or engage) takes effect immediately.\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport threading\n12\tfrom pathlib import Path\n13\tfrom typing import Optional\n14\t\n15\tfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n16\tfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n17\t\n18\tSIGNAL = \"governor.killswitch\"\n19\t_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\")\n20\t\n21\t# FIX 4 (audit CRITICAL): `is_engaged()` full-scans the spine on EVERY governor decision, so a batch of\n22\t# proposals is O(proposals × spine). Cache the authoritative verdict keyed by (resolved spine path,\n23\t# trusted pubkey) with the store's ROTATION-AWARE CHANGE TOKEN it was computed at. Every engage/release\n24\t# APPENDS a record → the token changes → the cache invalidates and we re-run the real, owner-signed-\n25\t# release-verifying scan. A matching token ⇒ no new records ⇒ the cached verdict is exact. The token\n26\t# (invariant 9 / A4) keys on the manifest generation + the resolved ACTIVE segment (size, inode), NOT a\n27\t# bare `store.path.stat()` — which would raise/freeze once a migration renames spine.jsonl away and then\n28\t# serve a STALE (un-halting) verdict indefinitely. Keyed on the pubkey too, so instances with different\n29\t# trust roots never share an entry. Shared across ALL callers on a path.\n30\t_STATE_CACHE: dict[tuple[str, Optional[str]], tuple[tuple, bool]] = {}\n31\t_CACHE_GUARD = threading.Lock()\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tclass KillSwitch:\n35\t    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n36\t        self.store = store\n37\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n38\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n39\t\n40\t    def engage(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n41\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"engaged\"}\n42\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n43\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n44\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n45\t\n46\t    def release(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n47\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"released\"}\n48\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n49\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n50\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n51\t\n52\t    def is_engaged(self) -> bool:\n53\t        \"\"\"Cheap, correct kill-switch verdict (FIX 4). Cheap when the spine is unchanged since the last\n54\t        authoritative scan (a matching rotation-aware change token); a changed token — a new engage/release\n55\t        record, or a migration/rotation that moved the active segment — re-runs the real scan below and\n56\t        refreshes the shared cache. Semantics are IDENTICAL to a fresh scan, and (unlike a bare file-size\n57\t        check) a migration that renames spine.jsonl away can never freeze the token and serve a stale\n58\t        un-halting verdict.\"\"\"\n59\t        key = (str(Path(self.store.path).resolve()), self.trusted_pubkey)\n60\t        token = self.store.change_token()\n61\t        with _CACHE_GUARD:\n62\t            cached = _STATE_CACHE.get(key)\n63\t            if cached is not None and cached[0] == token:\n64\t                return cached[1]\n65\t        engaged = self._scan_engaged()\n66\t        with _CACHE_GUARD:\n67\t            _STATE_CACHE[key] = (token, engaged)\n68\t        return engaged\n69\t\n70\t    def _scan_engaged(self) -> bool:\n71\t        \"\"\"The AUTHORITATIVE scan — unchanged semantics (not re-implemented): honor ANY engage (halting\n72\t        is fail-safe), and un-halt only on an OWNER-SIGNED release that verifies (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n73\t        engaged = False\n74\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n75\t            p = r.payload\n76\t            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL:\n77\t                continue\n78\t            state = p.get(\"state\")\n79\t            if state == \"engaged\":\n80\t                engaged = True                      # honor ANY engage — halting is fail-safe\n81\t            elif state == \"released\" and verify_signed(p, _CORE, self.trusted_pubkey):\n82\t                engaged = False                     # only an OWNER-SIGNED release un-halts (fail-closed)\n83\t        return engaged\n84\t"}]},"uuid":"516ae311-68dc-4d92-8d8e-c33701057c2a","timestamp":"2026-07-19T16:22:44.238Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"24358ec0-df9c-4cb4-90f8-48c8048def17","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"88053eec-1596-485a-9630-10dfb0690607","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"58479281-f0fc-437a-b3c2-51154bb8cfd1","agentId":"a3d0211873f170d2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016v6skxdowh2jBkpJUhjaCS","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SessionGate (Phase 8, WS-F, THE KEYSTONE) — the ONLY authority that injects input, and only inside\n2\tan owner-armed session. Two enforcement layers so a gesture can NEVER trigger an unintended action:\n3\t\n4\t  Layer 1 — the SESSION: `arm()` REQUIRES the owner key (it cannot be armed without the owner\n5\t  identity) and appends a signed `gesture.session_armed` record — the tamper-evident AUDIT proof-of-\n6\t  authorization, indicator-lit. The INJECTION gate itself is the live in-memory session, held ONLY by\n7\t  the owner's own running daemon process; it is BOUNDED by a TTL and ended by disarm / hand-loss /\n8\t  expiry. Injection is REFUSED with no live session.\n9\t\n10\t  Layer 2 — per intent, the tier is DERIVED from the WARDEN oracle (`hid.*` names): A1 (pointer\n11\t  move/click/scroll/drag) injects within the session; A2 (type/combo/app.launch) is QUEUED for a\n12\t  verified owner/device approval bound to `sha256(session_id|tool|args)` and NEVER auto-injected — so\n13\t  a gesture alone can never type a password or launch an app.\n14\t\n15\tOnly arm/disarm + DISCRETE actions (click/scroll/drag injected; type/launch queued) hit the spine —\n16\tper-frame pointer MOVES are telemetry (30 FPS records would DoS the append-only log).\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\timport math\n20\timport time\n21\timport uuid\n22\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n23\tfrom typing import Optional\n24\t\n25\t_KS_MIN_RESCAN = 0.05  # re-scan the kill-switch at most ~20x/s even on a churning spine (a cost floor)\n26\t\n27\tfrom ..agents.base import Tier\n28\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n29\tfrom .components import InputBackend\n30\tfrom .types import GestureIntent\n31\t\n32\tSESSION_ARMED = \"gesture.session_armed\"\n33\tSESSION_DISARMED = \"gesture.session_disarmed\"\n34\tACTION_SIGNAL = \"gesture.action\"\n35\tARM_REQUEST = \"gesture.arm_request\"\n36\t\n37\tMAX_DEVICE_TTL = 300.0        # a DEVICE-armed session is deliberately SHORTER than a local owner arm (1800)\n38\tARM_FRESHNESS = 30.0          # a device arm request must be recent (anti-stale / bounds the replay window)\n39\t_ARM_CORE = (\"signal\", \"device_id\", \"nonce\", \"ts\", \"ttl_seconds\")\n40\t\n41\tINTENT_TOOL = {\n42\t    \"move\": \"hid.pointer.move\", \"click\": \"hid.pointer.click\",\n43\t    \"scroll_left\": \"hid.pointer.scroll\", \"scroll_right\": \"hid.pointer.scroll\",\n44\t    \"drag\": \"hid.pointer.drag\", \"type\": \"hid.type\", \"combo\": \"hid.combo\", \"launch\": \"hid.app.launch\",\n45\t}\n46\t\n47\t\n48\tdef pending_device_arms(store, trusted_pubkey=None):\n49\t    \"\"\"ALL RECORDED device arm requests (written by the bridge's `submit_arm_request`) that have NOT yet\n50\t    armed a session, OLDEST-FIRST — candidates for `arm_by_device`, which RE-VERIFIES each fully (auth /\n51\t    freshness / kill-switch / replay / single-session / TTL). Returns a list (possibly empty). Oldest-first\n52\t    + returning ALL candidates means a newer STALE request never shadows an older still-valid one. This is\n53\t    the wiring that makes the remote-arm path reachable end-to-end (the gesture daemon consumes it).\"\"\"\n54\t    armed = set()\n55\t    cands = []\n56\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n57\t        p = r.payload\n58\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SESSION_ARMED and p.get(\"armed_by\") == \"device\":\n59\t            armed.add((p.get(\"device_pubkey\"), p.get(\"nonce\")))\n60\t        elif (p.get(\"signal\") == ARM_REQUEST and p.get(\"decision\") == \"auto\"\n61\t              and p.get(\"sig\") and p.get(\"pubkey\")):\n62\t            cands.append(dict(p))\n63\t    return [c for c in cands if (c.get(\"pubkey\"), c.get(\"nonce\")) not in armed]\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef arm_request_message(core: dict) -> bytes:\n67\t    \"\"\"The canonical bytes a device signs to request an arm — ONLY the signed core fields, so a caller\n68\t    cannot smuggle unsigned extras. Mirrors the approval/envelope canonical-json contract.\"\"\"\n69\t    from ..reuse import canonical_json\n70\t    m = canonical_json({k: core.get(k) for k in _ARM_CORE})\n71\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode(\"utf-8\")\n72\t\n73\t\n74\tdef sign_arm_request(device_key, *, device_id: str, nonce, ts: float, ttl_seconds: float) -> dict:\n75\t    \"\"\"Phone-side: build a device-signed arm request. The device signs with its OWN key (never the owner\n76\t    trust-root); the owner authorized this device once via the mesh ledger. This is the credential the\n77\t    bridge records and `SessionGate.arm_by_device` verifies.\"\"\"\n78\t    from ..reuse import sign\n79\t    core = {\"signal\": ARM_REQUEST, \"device_id\": device_id, \"nonce\": nonce, \"ts\": ts, \"ttl_seconds\": ttl_seconds}\n80\t    return {**core, \"pubkey\": device_key.public_key_b64,\n81\t            \"sig\": sign(device_key.private_key_b64, arm_request_message(core))}\n82\t\n83\t\n84\t@dataclass\n85\tclass Session:\n86\t    session_id: str\n87\t    live: bool = True\n88\t    expires_at: float = 0.0            # wallclock deadline — a session is BOUNDED, never indefinite\n89\t\n90\t    def expired(self, now: float) -> bool:\n91\t        return self.expires_at > 0.0 and now >= self.expires_at\n92\t\n93\t\n94\tclass SessionGate:\n95\t    def __init__(self, store, backend: InputBackend, *, classifier=None, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey=None):\n96\t        self.store = store\n97\t        self.backend = backend\n98\t        self._classifier = classifier\n99\t        self._owner_key = owner_key\n100\t        self._trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey\n101\t        self.session: Optional[Session] = None\n102\t        self._ks_engaged = False\n103\t        self._ks_token: tuple | None = None   # last-scanned spine change token; None forces a fresh check first\n104\t        self._ks_checked_at = -1e9\n105\t\n106\t    def _trusted(self):\n107\t        if self._trusted_pubkey is None:\n108\t            from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n109\t            self._trusted_pubkey = owner_pubkey()\n110\t        return self._trusted_pubkey\n111\t\n112\t    def _killswitch_engaged(self, now: float) -> bool:\n113\t        \"\"\"Kill-switch state for the per-intent gate. Re-scans the AUTHORITATIVE `KillSwitch` (which\n114\t        verifies the owner-signed release — never re-implemented here) ONLY when the store's ROTATION-AWARE\n115\t        change token has moved since the last scan: a panic APPENDS a record → the token changes → the halt\n116\t        is honored within ~1-2 frames (≈66 ms worst case), not a fixed 0.5 s. A pure-movement gesture\n117\t        appends nothing, so the token is unchanged and this is cheap with NO scan. Using the change token\n118\t        (invariant 9 / A4) rather than `store.path.stat()` is what keeps a panic observable AFTER a\n119\t        migration — a bare size check would raise/freeze once spine.jsonl is renamed away, silently\n120\t        stranding a device-armed session in the un-halted state. A short `_KS_MIN_RESCAN` floor caps a\n121\t        churning spine at per-floor scanning. The arm path checks FRESH.\"\"\"\n122\t        token = self.store.change_token()\n123\t        if token != self._ks_token and (now - self._ks_checked_at) >= _KS_MIN_RESCAN:\n124\t            from ..governor.killswitch import KillSwitch\n125\t            self._ks_engaged = KillSwitch(self.store).is_engaged()\n126\t            self._ks_token = token\n127\t            self._ks_checked_at = now\n128\t        return self._ks_engaged\n129\t\n130\t    def _cls(self):\n131\t        if self._classifier is None:\n132\t            from ..agents.kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n133\t            self._classifier = KernelClassifier()\n134\t        return self._classifier\n135\t\n136\t    def _backend_available(self) -> bool:\n137\t        \"\"\"True if the input backend can actually inject (SEAM-HONESTY). A backend with no\n138\t        `available()` — e.g. the `RecordingInputBackend` test spy, which faithfully records every\n139\t        call — is treated as able. A platform SEAM whose `available()` returns False (macOS/Windows\n140\t        native path not wired, or Linux with no ydotool/xdotool) is honestly INERT: it no-ops, so an\n141\t        injection is NEVER recorded/returned as done when it physically did nothing.\"\"\"\n142\t        probe = getattr(self.backend, \"available\", None)\n143\t        if probe is None:\n144\t            return True\n145\t        try:\n146\t            return bool(probe())\n147\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — a backend that can't even answer is treated as unavailable\n148\t            return False\n149\t\n150\t    # --- Layer 1: the owner-armed session ---------------------------------------------------------\n151\t    def arm(self, *, owner_key=None, ttl_seconds: float = 1800.0) -> Session:\n152\t        from ..governor.authn import signed_payload\n153\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair\n154\t        ok = owner_key if owner_key is not None else (self._owner_key or owner_keypair())\n155\t        if ok is None:\n156\t            raise RuntimeError(\"cannot arm a gesture session without the owner key (BLOCK-3)\")\n157\t        sid = uuid.uuid4().hex\n158\t        core = {\"signal\": SESSION_ARMED, \"session_id\": sid}\n159\t        self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n160\t                          payload={**signed_payload(core, ok), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n161\t                                   \"ttl_seconds\": ttl_seconds,\n162\t                                   \"summary\": \"gesture session ARMED (indicator lit — opt-in)\"})\n163\t        self.session = Session(sid, live=True, expires_at=time.time() + ttl_seconds)\n164\t        return self.session\n165\t\n166\t    def disarm(self) -> None:\n167\t        if self.session is not None:\n168\t            self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n169\t                              payload={\"signal\": SESSION_DISARMED, \"session_id\": self.session.session_id,\n170\t                                       \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\", \"summary\": \"gesture session DISARMED\"})\n171\t            self.session.live = False\n172\t        self.session = None\n173\t\n174\t    # --- Layer 1b: DEVICE-signed remote arm (a deliberate, reviewed trust-widening) ----------------\n175\t    def arm_by_device(self, request: dict, *, now: Optional[float] = None) -> Optional[Session]:\n176\t        \"\"\"Arm on a VERIFIED, owner-authorized DEVICE signature (SIGIL §remote-arm). Every downstream\n177\t        bound is UNCHANGED — the A1-inject/A2-queue tier gate, the TTL, hand-loss/expiry disarm, and an\n178\t        owner disarm/kill that always wins. Fail-closed at each step; a refusal is recorded, never armed.\"\"\"\n179\t        from ..governor.killswitch import KillSwitch\n180\t        from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n181\t        from ..reuse import verify_one\n182\t        now = time.time() if now is None else now\n183\t        # (1) kill-switch FIRST — an owner halt (incl. a phone panic) beats any arm.\n184\t        if KillSwitch(self.store).is_engaged():\n185\t            self._refuse_arm(request, \"kill-switch engaged\")\n186\t            return None\n187\t        # (2) owner-minted device key + valid signature over the signed core (RP-APPROVAL-2: the\n188\t        #     authorized set is owner-signed only, so a merely-presented key is never trusted).\n189\t        if request.get(\"signal\") != ARM_REQUEST:\n190\t            self._refuse_arm(request, \"not an arm request\")\n191\t            return None\n192\t        pub = request.get(\"pubkey\")\n193\t        authorized = authorized_devices(self.store, self._trusted())\n194\t        core = {k: request.get(k) for k in _ARM_CORE}\n195\t        try:                                            # verify_one RAISES on a malformed-length sig — treat as refusal\n196\t            sig_ok = bool(pub) and pub in authorized and verify_one(pub, arm_request_message(core), request.get(\"sig\", \"\"))\n197\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — any crypto/decoding error is a fail-closed refusal, never a crash\n198\t            sig_ok = False\n199\t        if not sig_ok:\n200\t            self._refuse_arm(request, \"unauthorized or invalid device signature\")\n201\t            return None\n202\t        # (3) freshness — bounds the replay window and rejects a stale captured request. Written\n203\t        #     fail-CLOSED for non-finite input: `nan > 30` is False, so a signed NaN ts would slip a\n204\t        #     `>`-style gate; require finite AND within-window explicitly.\n205\t        try:\n206\t            # request is a caller-supplied dict; a missing/None ts hits the except below (fail-closed)\n207\t            ts = float(request.get(\"ts\"))  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n208\t        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n209\t            self._refuse_arm(request, \"missing/invalid timestamp\")\n210\t            return None\n211\t        if not math.isfinite(ts) or not (abs(now - ts) <= ARM_FRESHNESS):\n212\t            self._refuse_arm(request, \"stale or non-finite timestamp\")\n213\t            return None\n214\t        # (4) replay — this (device, nonce) must not have armed before (the append-only spine is witness).\n215\t        nonce = request.get(\"nonce\")\n216\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n217\t            p = r.payload\n218\t            if (p.get(\"signal\") == SESSION_ARMED and p.get(\"armed_by\") == \"device\"\n219\t                    and p.get(\"device_pubkey\") == pub and p.get(\"nonce\") == nonce):\n220\t                self._refuse_arm(request, \"replayed arm nonce\")\n221\t                return None\n222\t        # (5) single live session — a device arm NEVER displaces an existing/owner session.\n223\t        if self.session is not None and self.session.live and not self.session.expired(now):\n224\t            self._refuse_arm(request, \"a gesture session is already live\")\n225\t            return None\n226\t        # (6) TTL clamp — shorter than a local owner arm; a deliberate trust-narrowing inside the widening.\n227\t        #     Reject non-finite/non-positive BEFORE the min() (min(nan,300)=nan → a never-expiring session).\n228\t        try:\n229\t            raw_ttl = float(request.get(\"ttl_seconds\", MAX_DEVICE_TTL))\n230\t        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n231\t            self._refuse_arm(request, \"invalid ttl\")\n232\t            return None\n233\t        if not math.isfinite(raw_ttl) or raw_ttl <= 0:\n234\t            self._refuse_arm(request, \"non-finite or non-positive ttl\")\n235\t            return None\n236\t        ttl = min(raw_ttl, MAX_DEVICE_TTL)\n237\t        sid = uuid.uuid4().hex\n238\t        # The record's AUTHORIZATION is the verified device signature — self-verifying vs the owner-signed\n239\t        # device ledger; actor=DEVICE mirrors submit_device_approval. Store the ORIGINAL SIGNED core\n240\t        # fields (so the sig re-verifies for ANY ttl, incl. a clamped one) + the effective TTL separately,\n241\t        # and ONLY known fields (never `**request` — no unsigned extras persisted).\n242\t        self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n243\t                          payload={\"signal\": SESSION_ARMED, \"session_id\": sid, \"armed_by\": \"device\",\n244\t                                   \"device_id\": core[\"device_id\"], \"device_pubkey\": pub,\n245\t                                   \"nonce\": core[\"nonce\"], \"ts\": core[\"ts\"],\n246\t                                   \"ttl_seconds\": core[\"ttl_seconds\"],   # ORIGINAL signed value → sig re-verifies\n247\t                                   \"effective_ttl\": ttl,                 # the CLAMPED ttl actually enforced\n248\t                                   \"sig\": request.get(\"sig\"), \"pubkey\": pub, \"authorization\": \"device-signed\",\n249\t                                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n250\t                                   \"summary\": \"gesture session ARMED by authorized device (remote)\"})\n251\t        self.session = Session(sid, live=True, expires_at=now + ttl)\n252\t        return self.session\n253\t\n254\t    def _refuse_arm(self, request: dict, reason: str) -> None:\n255\t        self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n256\t                          payload={\"signal\": ARM_REQUEST, \"decision\": \"refused\", \"tier\": \"A0\",\n257\t                                   \"device_id\": request.get(\"device_id\"), \"reason\": reason,\n258\t                                   \"summary\": f\"device gesture arm refused: {reason}\"})\n259\t        return None\n260\t\n261\t    # --- Layer 2: per-intent tier gate ------------------------------------------------------------\n262\t    def handle(self, intent: GestureIntent) -> dict:\n263\t        \"\"\"Authorize + act on ONE intent. Returns a verdict dict. Injection ONLY inside a live session\n264\t        and ONLY for A1 tools; A2+ queues for approval and injects NOTHING; a danger name → refused.\"\"\"\n265\t        if intent.kind == \"hand_lost\":\n266\t            self.disarm()\n267\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"hand lost — session disarmed\"}\n268\t        # An owner halt (incl. a phone panic_engage) neuters injection mid-session — critical for a\n269\t        # device-armed session the owner may not be watching. `_killswitch_engaged` only re-scans when the\n270\t        # spine GREW (a panic appends → detected within ~1-2 frames), so a 30fps movement loop never\n271\t        # re-scans per frame; latency is bounded by the rescan floor, not a fixed TTL.\n272\t        if self._killswitch_engaged(time.time()):\n273\t            self.disarm()\n274\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"kill-switch engaged — session disarmed\"}\n275\t        if self.session is None or not self.session.live:\n276\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"no armed session — injection refused\"}\n277\t        if self.session.expired(time.time()):             # BLOCK-2: a session is bounded, never indefinite\n278\t            self.disarm()\n279\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"session expired — disarmed\"}\n280\t        tool = INTENT_TOOL.get(intent.kind)\n281\t        if tool is None:\n282\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": f\"unknown intent {intent.kind}\"}\n283\t        tier = self._cls().classify(tool)                 # DERIVED, fail-closed A3 on any error\n284\t        args = f\"{intent.dx:.4f},{intent.dy:.4f},{intent.arg}\"\n285\t        if tier <= Tier.A1:\n286\t            acted = self._backend_available()   # a platform SEAM (available()==False) is honestly inert\n287\t            self._inject(intent)                # an inert backend no-ops → NOTHING is physically injected\n288\t            # BLOCK-1: log the DISCRETE A1 injections that actually take effect (click/scroll/drag);\n289\t            # per-frame `move` is telemetry (logging it at 30 FPS would DoS the append-only spine).\n290\t            # SEAM-HONESTY: never let the audit log CLAIM an injection the backend didn't perform —\n291\t            # when the backend is inert, record honestly (`backend_inert: True`, \"NOT injected\"), and\n292\t            # return injected=False. The A2-queue path below is unchanged.\n293\t            if intent.kind != \"move\":\n294\t                payload = {\"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"auto\", \"tier\": tier.label(),\n295\t                           \"tool\": tool, \"session_id\": self.session.session_id,\n296\t                           \"summary\": (f\"gesture {tool} injected\" if acted\n297\t                                       else f\"gesture {tool} NOT injected — input backend inert (no-op)\")}\n298\t                if not acted:\n299\t                    payload[\"backend_inert\"] = True\n300\t                self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n301\t            verdict = {\"injected\": bool(acted), \"tier\": tier.label(), \"tool\": tool}\n302\t            if not acted:\n303\t                verdict[\"backend_inert\"] = True\n304\t                verdict[\"reason\"] = \"input backend inert — no physical injection performed\"\n305\t            return verdict\n306\t        # A2/A3 → QUEUE an approval bound to this exact action; inject NOTHING\n307\t        token = sha256_hex(f\"{self.session.session_id}|{tool}|{args}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n308\t        seq = self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"PERCEPTION\",\n309\t                                payload={\"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"queued\",\n310\t                                         \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\", \"tier\": tier.label(),\n311\t                                         \"tool\": tool, \"action_token\": token, \"args\": args,\n312\t                                         \"subject\": f\"gesture action {tool} (awaiting approval)\"})\n313\t        return {\"injected\": False, \"queued\": seq, \"tier\": tier.label(), \"tool\": tool, \"action_token\": token}\n314\t\n315\t    def _inject(self, intent: GestureIntent) -> None:\n316\t        b = self.backend\n317\t        if intent.kind == \"move\" or intent.kind == \"drag\":\n318\t            b.move(intent.dx, intent.dy)\n319\t        elif intent.kind == \"click\":\n320\t            b.click()\n321\t        elif intent.kind == \"scroll_left\":\n322\t            b.scroll(-1, 0)\n323\t        elif intent.kind == \"scroll_right\":\n324\t            b.scroll(1, 0)\n325\t"}]},"uuid":"3ed323b0-1681-4818-af7e-41a8599686e2","timestamp":"2026-07-19T16:22:44.880Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"88053eec-1596-485a-9630-10dfb0690607","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"ea304f02-80d6-4acf-a337-ef23c9cf1462","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"58479281-f0fc-437a-b3c2-51154bb8cfd1","agentId":"a3d0211873f170d2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QXSqnaqokKE2Z8bbfdASPd","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Durable, atomic file replacement — the one implementation the spine's crash-safe swaps share.\n2\t\n3\tExtracted verbatim from `checkpoint._atomic_write_text` (FIX 3) so the signed head, the manifest, and\n4\tany future single-file cutover all use ONE audited routine rather than drifting copies. The contract:\n5\ta reader observes either the whole old file or the whole new file (never a torn one), and the new\n6\tcontent survives a crash at any point — a crash mid-write leaves the PREVIOUS valid file intact.\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport os\n11\timport tempfile\n12\tfrom pathlib import Path\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tdef fsync_dir(directory: Path | str) -> None:\n16\t    \"\"\"fsync a directory so a rename/create inside it is itself durable across a crash. A no-op-on-error\n17\t    on filesystems that don't support directory fsync (the rename still lands; only its durability window\n18\t    widens).\"\"\"\n19\t    try:\n20\t        dfd = os.open(str(directory), os.O_DIRECTORY)\n21\t        try:\n22\t            os.fsync(dfd)\n23\t        finally:\n24\t            os.close(dfd)\n25\t    except OSError:  # pragma: no cover — dir fsync unsupported on some filesystems\n26\t        pass\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tdef atomic_write_text(path: Path | str, data: str, *, prefix: str = \".tmp-\") -> None:\n30\t    \"\"\"Durably + atomically replace `path` with `data`: write a temp file in the SAME dir, fsync it,\n31\t    `os.replace()` over the target (atomic on POSIX), then fsync the directory so the rename survives a\n32\t    crash. A crash at any point leaves the previous valid file intact, never a partially-written one.\"\"\"\n33\t    path = Path(path)\n34\t    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n35\t    fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=str(path.parent), prefix=prefix, suffix=\".tmp\")\n36\t    try:\n37\t        with os.fdopen(fd, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n38\t            f.write(data)\n39\t            f.flush()\n40\t            os.fsync(f.fileno())\n41\t        os.replace(tmp, path)\n42\t    except BaseException:\n43\t        try:\n44\t            os.unlink(tmp)\n45\t        except OSError:\n46\t            pass\n47\t        raise\n48\t    fsync_dir(path.parent)\n49\t"}]},"uuid":"af31c2e7-2239-4de2-bb30-393d57771480","timestamp":"2026-07-19T16:22:44.883Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ea304f02-80d6-4acf-a337-ef23c9cf1462","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Behavior stays BYTE-IDENTICAL until an explicit migrate() — with no\n    manifest the store reads/writes the legacy single file in place.\n    \n    - spine/atomicio.py (new): the one shared temp→fsync→os.replace→dir-fsync routine; checkpoint's\n      _atomic_write_text delegates to it (no drift).\n    - spine/manifest.py (new): Segment/Manifest models (unsigned, non-load-bearing — a doctored manifest\n      fails CLOSED; segment `file` is validated to a contained relative path so reads can't be steered at\n      /etc/*), SpineLayout NAMESPACED BY the data-file stem (spine.manifest.json / spine.lock /\n      spine.segments/) so co-located stores never collide, atomic read/write.\n    - config.py: layout is derived by SpineLayout, not fixed constants (no drift / no \"dir is private\" bug).\n    - store.py: DATA I/O targets self._active (manifest active, or legacy file in place); cross-process flock\n      moved to the inode-stable spine.lock (survives os.replace); in-process RLock stays keyed on the stable\n      self.path (the lock envelope.consume shares with append is the same object across a rotation). append\n      re-resolves the active UNDER the lock AND completes an interrupted migration there, so a live appender\n      never resurrects spine.jsonl / forks. migrate() renames spine.jsonl -> seg-0 (O(1), fsync BOTH dirs) +\n      publishes a manifest; a reconciler (construction AND live-append paths) closes the rename↔manifest\n      crash window. change_token() (unified A4 epoch) replaces the size-only kill-switch tokens in\n      killswitch.py and gesture/session.py — a migration that renames spine.jsonl away can no longer freeze a\n      stale (un-halting) verdict. Reads re-resolve a vanished active for the migrate instant (F3).\n      Degenerate \"manifest with no active\" RAISES rather than resurrecting spine.jsonl (Slice-3 landmine).\n    \n    Dual adversarial review (concurrency/durability + behavior-parity) BEFORE building further: caught and\n    fixed a CRITICAL live-appender-resurrects-spine.jsonl fork (reconciler now runs on the append path too),\n    a layout cross-store contamination (stem namespacing), a kill-switch un-halting regression after migrate\n    (change_token), a half-fsynced cross-dir rename, a manifest path-escape, and a no-active resurrection\n    landmine — each with a regression test.\n    \n    Tests (test_spine_rotation.py, 11): legacy byte-identical; RLock stable across migration; migrate\n    preserves chain/next_seq/verify; idempotent; fresh-migrate; orphan reconcile (construction + live\n    appender); change_token moves on append/migrate; kill-switch panic observed after migration; manifest\n    path-escape rejected; split-brain re-resolve.\n    \n    359 passed (348 + 11); ruff + mypy clean. These tests pin the Slice-0 invariants:\n6\t  - a legacy store is byte-identical (no manifest / segments dir created; data stays in spine.jsonl);\n7\t  - the in-process RLock is keyed on the STABLE store.path and survives a migration (invariant 14 — the\n8\t    lock `envelope.consume` shares with `append` is the same object across a rotation);\n9\t  - migrate() renames spine.jsonl → segments/seg-00000000.jsonl (O(1)) + publishes a manifest, preserving\n10\t    the chain, next_seq, and verify();\n11\t  - a STALE appender constructed before a concurrent migrate() re-resolves the active segment under the\n12\t    lock and never resurrects spine.jsonl (the split-brain the two-step migration guards against).\n13\tRun: ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_spine_rotation.py -q\n14\t\"\"\"\n15\timport os\n16\timport tempfile\n17\tfrom pathlib import Path\n18\t\n19\tfrom sigil.spine.manifest import read_manifest, SpineLayout\n20\tfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore, spine_lock\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tdef _fresh_dir(prefix: str) -> Path:\n24\t    return Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=prefix))\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef _append_n(store: SpineStore, n: int, start: int = 0) -> None:\n28\t    for i in range(start, start + n):\n29\t        store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": i})\n30\t\n31\t\n32\tdef test_legacy_store_is_byte_identical():\n33\t    \"\"\"No manifest ⇒ active target IS the legacy single file; no manifest/segments artifacts appear.\"\"\"\n34\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-legacy-\")\n35\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n36\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n37\t    _append_n(s, 4)\n38\t    lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\n39\t    assert s._active == p, \"legacy store must append/read in place at spine.jsonl\"\n40\t    assert p.exists()\n41\t    assert not lay.manifest_path.exists(), \"no manifest is written until an explicit migrate()\"\n42\t    assert not lay.segments_dir.exists(), \"segments/ is not created for a legacy/fresh store\"\n43\t    ok, reason = s.verify()\n44\t    assert ok, reason\n45\t    assert [r.seq for r in s.iter_records()] == [0, 1, 2, 3]\n46\t\n47\t\n48\tdef test_rlock_keyed_on_stable_path_and_survives_migration():\n49\t    \"\"\"Invariant 14: the RLock is keyed on the stable store.path, so the object `append` and\n50\t    `envelope.consume` both take via spine_lock(store.path) is identical — and stays identical across a\n51\t    migration (store.path never changes, even though spine.jsonl is renamed away).\"\"\"\n52\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-rlock-\")\n53\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n54\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n55\t    _append_n(s, 2)\n56\t    lock_before = spine_lock(s.path)\n57\t    assert spine_lock(s.path) is lock_before, \"same path ⇒ same RLock object\"\n58\t    assert s.migrate() is True\n59\t    assert s.path == p, \"store.path (the lock/identity token) is unchanged by migration\"\n60\t    assert spine_lock(s.path) is lock_before, \"the RLock survives the migration unchanged\"\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tdef test_migrate_preserves_chain_and_moves_to_segment():\n64\t    d = assert (lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\").exists()\n74\t    assert lay.manifest_path.exists()\n75\t\n76\t    # reads identical, chain intact, tip preserved\n77\t    assert [(r.seq, r.entry_hash, r.payload[\"n\"]) for r in s.iter_records()] == before\n78\t    ok, reason = s.verify()\n79\t    assert ok, reason\n80\t    assert s.next_seq == 5\n81\t\n82\t    # a FRESH instance fresh.get(2) is not None and fresh.get(2).payload[\"n\"] == 2\n86\t\n87\t    # appends continue cleanly with no seq reuse\n88\t    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": test_orphan_migration_is_reconciled():\n122\t    \"\"\"The judge-flagged migration crash window: a crash AFTER the atomic rename but BEFORE the manifest\n123\t    write leaves seg-0 present, spine.jsonl gone, no manifest. __init__ must reconcile\n139\t    assert lay.manifest_path.exists(), \"reconciler publishes the manifest to complete the migration\"\n140\t    assert [r.seq for r in s2.iter_records()] == before, \"records recovered, NOT hidden as an empty spine\"\n141\t    ok, reason = s2.verify()\n142\t    assert ok, reason\n143\t\n144\t\n145\tdef test_live_appender_completes_orphan_migration_no_resurrect():\n146\t    \"\"\"The CRITICAL review finding: a long-lived appender (e.g. the bridge) that never re-constructed its\n147\t    store, appending AFTER another process crashed mid-migrate(), must COMPLETE the orphan under the lock\n148\t    and write to the migrated segment — never re-create spine.jsonl and fork the log.\"\"\"\n149\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-orphanlive-\")\n150\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n151\t    lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\n152\t    a = SpineStore(p)                                      # long-lived appender, legacy _active == spine.jsonl\n153\t    _append_n(a, 3)\n154\t    assert a._active == p\n155\t\n156\t    # simulate a crash mid-migrate() in ANOTHER process: rename done, manifest NOT written\n157\t    lay.segments_dir.mkdir()\n158\t    os.replace(p, lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\")\n159\t    assert not p.exists()\n160\t\n161\t    a.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 3})   # A never saw the migration\n162\t    assert not p.exists(), \"the live appender must NOT resurrect spine.jsonl (fork)\"\n163\t    assert lay.manifest_path.exists(), \"the live appender completed the interrupted migration under the lock\"\n164\t    recs = [r.payload[\"n\"] for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()]\n165\t    assert recs == [0, 1, 2, 3], f\"no fork — all records in the migrated segment: {recs}\"\n166\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n167\t    assert ok, reason\n168\t\n169\t\n170\tdef test_change_token_reflects_append_and_migration():\n171\t    \"\"\"change_token() (the unified A4 epoch) must move on every append, on migration, and on a post-\n172\t    migration append — so a bare file-size check that would freeze once spine.jsonl is renamed away is\n173\t    replaced by a token that keeps changing.\"\"\"\n174\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-tok-\")\n175\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n176\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n177\t    t0 = s.change_token()\n178\t    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 0})\n179\t    t1 = s.change_token()\n180\t    assert t1 != t0, \"append changes the token\"\n181\t    s.migrate()\n182\t    t2 = s.change_token()\n183\t    assert t2 != t1, \"migration changes the token (generation + active path)\"\n184\t    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 1})\n185\t    assert s.change_token() != t2, \"append after migration changes the token\"\n186\t\n187\t\n188\tdef test_gesture_killswitch_panic_observed_after_migration():\n189\t    \"\"\"BLOCK-2 end-to-end: a kill-switch PANIC engaged AFTER a migration must be observed by a running\n190\t    gesture session. The old size-token froze on OSError once spine.jsonl was renamed away — stranding a\n191\t    device-armed session un-halted. The change token keeps the panic observable.\"\"\"\n192\t    from sigil.gesture.session import SessionGate\n193\t\n194\t    class _NullBackend:\n195\t        def move(self, *a, **k): pass\n196\t        def click(self, *a, **k): pass\n197\t        def type_text(self, *a, **k): pass\n198\t        def launch(self, *a, **k): pass\n199\t\n200\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-kssess-\")\n201\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n202\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n203\t    g = SessionGate(s, _NullBackend())\n204\t    assert g._killswitch_engaged(now=1.0) is False\n205\t    s.migrate()                                            # spine.jsonl renamed away\n206\t    from sigil.governor.killswitch import KillSwitch\n207\t    KillSwitch(s).engage(by=\"owner\", reason=\"post-migration panic\")\n208\t    assert g._killswitch_engaged(now=100.0) is True, \\\n209\t        \"a panic after migration must be observed (a frozen size token would strand the session un-halted)\"\n210\t\n211\t\n212\tdef test_manifest_rejects_path_escaping_segment_file():\n213\t    \"\"\"MEDIUM-1: a doctored manifest whose segment `file` escapes the spine dir (absolute or `..`) is\n214\t    rejected at the model boundary, because get()/iter/tail open segment files without running verify().\"\"\"\n215\t    import pytest\n216\t    from pydantic import ValidationError\n217\t\n218\t    from sigil.spine.manifest import Segment\n219\t\n220\t    for bad in (\"/etc/hostname\", \"../../etc/hostname\", \"../secret.jsonl\"):\n221\t        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):\n222\t            Segment(id=0, file=bad, first_seq=0)\n223\t    Segment(id=0, file=\"spine.segments/seg-00000000.jsonl\", first_seq=0)   # a contained relative path is fine\n224\t\n225\t\n226\tdef test_stale_appender_reresolves_no_split_brain():\n227\t    \"\"\"The core split-brain test. Instance A appends in legacy mode; instance B migrates; A appends\n228\t    again. A MUST re-resolve the active segment under the lock and write to the migrated segment — never\n229\t    resurrect spine.jsonl and fork the chain.\"\"\"\n230\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-sb-\")\n231\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n232\t    a = SpineStore(p)\n233\t    a.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 0})\n234\t    assert a._active == p                                   # A cached the legacy path\n235\t\n236\t    b = SpineStore(p)                                       # \"another process\"\n237\t    assert b.migrate() is True                              # p is renamed → seg-0; A's cached _active is now stale\n238\t\n239\t    a.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 1})   # A must re-resolve under the lock\n240\t    assert not p.exists(), \"a stale appender must not resurrect spine.jsonl (split-brain / fork)\"\n241\t\n242\t    recs = [r.payload[\"n\"] for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()]\n243\t    assert recs == [0, 1], f\"both records land in the migrated segment, no fork: {recs}\"\n244\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n245\t    assert ok, reason\n246\t    seqs = [r.seq for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()]\n247\t    assert seqs == [0, 1], f\"no seq reuse across the migration seam: {seqs}\"\n248\t"}]},"uuid":"83410d70-a829-468b-90c3-4bf9ec4de6d9","timestamp":"2026-07-19T16:28:35.149Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"673bdad1-78f2-4bb9-a8f8-c3003b51062e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

PY=$( [ -x ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python ] && echo ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python || echo python3 ); echo \"PY=$PY\"; cat > /tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/repro.py <<'EOF'\nimport os, tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\nfrom sigil.spine.manifest import SpineLayout\nfrom sigil.governor.killswitch import KillSwitch, _STATE_CACHE\n\ndef mid_migrate_window(store, p, lay):\n    \"\"\"Put the ON-DISK state into the pre-manifest migrate window WITHOUT reconstructing `store`\n    (so store._active stays the stale legacy spine.jsonl, exactly as a long-lived reader would see it).\n    This is the exact state between os.replace() and write_manifest() inside migrate().\"\"\"\n    lay.segments_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    os.replace(p, lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\")  # atomic rename; spine.jsonl gone, seg-0 present\n    assert not p.exists() and (lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\").exists()\n    assert not lay.manifest_path.exists()   # manifest not yet published — THE window\n\n# ---- Repro 1: false-EMPTY read on a live reader during the window ----\nd = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"win1-\")); p = d/\"spine.jsonl\"; lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\ns = SpineStore(p)\nfor i in range(4):\n    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": i})\nprint(\"before window: iter_records seqs =\", [r.seq for r in s.iter_records()], \" count =\", s.count())\nmid_migrate_window(s, p, lay)\nprint(\"DURING window: iter_records seqs =\", [r.seq for r in s.iter_records()], \" count =\", s.count(),\n      \"  <-- 4 records on disk in seg-0, reader sees EMPTY\")\n\n# ---- Repro 2: FileNotFoundError crash on the indexed get() path during the window ----\nd2 = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"win2-\")); p2 = d2/\"spine.jsonl\"; lay2 = SpineLayout.for_path(p2)\ns2 = SpineStore(p2)\nfor i in range(4):\n    s2.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": i})\n# Build the index while spine.jsonl still exists (simulates get() passing its first _read_target().exists()\n# check, THEN os.replace landing before _scan_from's open). We call _ensure_index after entering the window\n# but with _index_built already False and size measured on the (now vanished) resolved path:\nassert s2.get(1) is not None      # index built over spine.jsonl\ns2._invalidate_index()            # force a rebuild on the next read (as a concurrent append's refresh would)\nmid_migrate_window(s2, p2, lay2)\n# simulate the TOCTOU: get()'s line-420 existence check passed a moment ago; now the scan runs in the window\ntry:\n    r = s2.get(1)\n    print(\"Repro2 get(1) ->\", r, \" (returned None, no crash)\")\nexcept FileNotFoundError as e:\n    print(\"Repro2 get(1) RAISED FileNotFoundError:\", e, \" <-- crash on indexed read path\")\n\n# ---- Repro 3: kill-switch UN-HALT during the window ----\n_STATE_CACHE.clear()\nd3 = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"win3-\")); p3 = d3/\"spine.jsonl\"; lay3 = SpineLayout.for_path(p3)\ns3 = SpineStore(p3)\nks = KillSwitch(s3)\nks.engage(by=\"owner\", reason=\"halt the mesh\")\nprint(\"engaged, before window: is_engaged =\", KillSwitch(s3).is_engaged())\n_STATE_CACHE.clear()  # drop the cached True (a fresh governor decision / different instance)\nmid_migrate_window(s3, p3, lay3)\nprint(\"DURING window:          is_engaged =\", KillSwitch(s3).is_engaged(),\n      \"  <-- kill-switch WAS engaged; reader sees NOT engaged => transient UN-HALT\")\nprint(\"change_token in window  =\", s3.change_token())\nEOF\n$PY /tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/repro.py","description":"Repro false-empty/crash/un-halt during migrate window"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":117223,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":117223,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdBjQok7TkRzGrA2tbJTn","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"705ca14b-5a65-47e2-a024-86474b8a5962","timestamp":"2026-07-19T16:29:09.973Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"1b3c81a5-fc2d-439b-ae86-a35cb9547c2d","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a3d0211873f170d2c","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdBjbCoDhef12CnooYXd9","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"All 94 related tests pass. I have everything I need. Let me consolidate the findings.\n\n## RED-PEN RE-CHECK — SIGIL spine rotation Slice 0 (commit `0b4ac6a`)\n\nI read the full diff and all four touched files plus `atomicio.py`, `manifest.py`, `config.py`, the 11 rotation tests, and grepped every consumer of the changed interfaces. I then built runnable repros for the one class of defect I found. **11/11 rotation tests and 94/94 spine+governor+gesture+envelope tests pass.**\n\nVerdict: **5 of the 6 fixes hold. F3 is incomplete — it introduced/left open a real defect.** One Medium finding survives, plus three Low/Note items.\n\n---\n\n### FINDING 1 (Medium) — F3 is incomplete: the *pre-manifest* half of the migrate window still yields false-empty reads and one crash path\n\n`sigil/spine/store.py:188` `_read_target()` closes the *post-manifest* window (active vanished, manifest already published → resolve seg-0 from the manifest). It does **nothing** for the *pre-manifest* window inside `migrate()` — the interval between `os.replace(self.path, seg0_abs)` (`store.py:325`) and the completion of `write_manifest(...)` (`store.py:330`). During that interval spine.jsonl is gone, seg-0 exists, and **no manifest exists yet**, so `_read_target()` falls through to `return a` (the vanished legacy path). That window spans two `fsync_dir` calls plus a full `atomic_write_text` (its own fsync + rename + dir-fsync) — not negligible under load. Reads take **no lock** (only `migrate()`/`append()` hold `_crossproc_lock`), so any concurrent reader observes it. This directly contradicts the F3 docstring's own claim to \"prevent a false-EMPTY read during the migrate instant.\"\n\nTwo consequences, both reproduced:\n\n- **False-empty read** (`iter_records`/`count`/`entries`/`verify`/`tail`, all lock-free). With 4 records on disk in seg-0, a live reader sees 0. Concretely:\n  - kill-switch scan (`killswitch.py:74` `_scan_engaged`) → `engaged=False` → **transient un-halt of an engaged mesh** (the exact un-halting direction the whole change_token fix targets).\n  - envelope nonce-highwater scan → highwater resets to 0 → **replay-floor regression** (the docstring names this specific risk).\n\n  Repro output:\n  ```\n  before window: iter_records seqs = [0, 1, 2, 3]  count = 4\n  DURING window: iter_records seqs = []  count = 0   <-- 4 records on disk in seg-0, reader sees EMPTY\n  engaged, before window: is_engaged = True\n  DURING window:          is_engaged = False         <-- transient UN-HALT\n  ```\n\n- **Unhandled `FileNotFoundError` crash on the indexed read path** (`get()`, `iter_records(since_seq>=0)`, `_start_offset_for`). `_ensure_index` measures `size` from one `_read_target()` (`store.py:534-536`) but `_scan_from` opens a *second* `_read_target()` (`store.py:560`). If `os.replace` lands between them, `size>0` was measured on the present spine.jsonl but the open hits the vanished path — and the `try/except OSError` in `_ensure_index` does **not** wrap `_scan_from`. Forcing that exact interleaving:\n  ```\n  CRASH: FileNotFoundError out of the indexed read path: FileNotFoundError(2, 'No such file or directory')\n  ```\n\nTrigger: run `migrate()` on a live mesh (the design's stated goal) with any concurrent governor/nonce/read activity. Root cause is singular: `_read_target` should fall back to seg-0 directly when spine.jsonl has vanished and no manifest is present yet (the orphan/reconcile signature), instead of returning the vanished legacy path. (Reconciliation only runs at construction or under `append`'s lock — it never helps a lock-free reader.)\n\n---\n\n### FINDING 2 (Low) — \"byte-identical legacy\" is false at the directory level: a `spine.lock` file now appears\n\n`append()` now wraps every write in `_crossproc_lock()`, which `os.open(..., O_CREAT)`s the lockfile (`store.py:220`, `<stem>.lock` in the spine dir). Pre-rotation, a legacy append used `flock` on the data fd and created **no** extra file. Now the first legacy append leaves `['spine.jsonl', 'spine.lock']` where it was `['spine.jsonl']`. Record *content* is byte-identical (verified), but the headline \"byte-identical\" and the \"no manifest/segments artifacts appear\" test (`test_legacy_store_is_byte_identical`) don't cover the lockfile. Benign, but a real deviation from the stated invariant. (Also: every legacy append now does 2 extra stats — `read_manifest`'s `exists()` + `_complete`'s `seg0.exists()` — cost, not correctness.)\n\n### FINDING 3 (Note) — stem-only namespacing under-delivers \"co-located stores never collide\"\n\n`SpineLayout.for_path` keys artifacts on `p.stem` (`manifest.py:110`). Two data files sharing a stem but differing in extension (`spine.jsonl` vs `spine.json`) map to the **same** `.manifest.json`/`.lock`/`.segments/` — the very cross-store contamination the change claims to fix, just narrowed. Pathological data-file names (`<stem>.segments`) would make `segments_dir == data_path` and break `migrate()`'s `mkdir`. Not a risk for the single production spine (`tempfile.mktemp` stems are unique, tests pass), but the \"never collide\" claim is overstated.\n\n### FINDING 4 (Note, pre-existing) — the \"unified epoch token\" doesn't close the A4 size-collision *within a single segment*\n\nIn the no-migrate case, `change_token()` (`store.py:288`) degenerates to `(const gen, const path, raw_size, const ino)` — effectively size-only, because it reads **raw** `st_size` (torn tail included). An append that truncates a torn tail of length exactly equal to the new record's length leaves `st_size` unchanged → identical token → the kill-switch serves the stale cached verdict (un-halt direction). This is **pre-existing** (old code keyed on the same raw `st_size`) and negligible-probability (crash + exact byte-length coincidence), but the fix only eliminated A4 *across rotation*, not within one segment. Worth recording as a known residual, not a regression.\n\n---\n\n### Fixes that hold (attested)\n\n- **F1 (a)/(c):** live-appender orphan completion writes to seg-0 with no spine.jsonl resurrection (test + trace); `_complete_orphan_migration_locked`'s guard `seg0.exists() and not self.path.exists()` means pure-legacy appends never write a manifest (only 2 extra stats); `write_manifest` never re-enters the flock — no deadlock.\n- **F1 (b):** with both spine.jsonl and seg-0 present, `_complete` correctly *skips* (guard is False). A fresh store adopting a stray seg-0 is safe recovery, because stem-namespacing guarantees seg-0 belongs to this store's identity.\n- **BLOCK-2 (a)/(d):** panic-after-migration is observed (`test_gesture_killswitch_panic_observed_after_migration`); `_STATE_CACHE` is read only in `killswitch.py`, so the `(int,bool)→(tuple,bool)` value-type change is contained; the fresh-per-call manifest read is cost, not a correctness bug (it's what makes a cross-process migrate visible).\n- **F2:** fsync ordering (os.replace → fsync source dir → fsync target dir → `write_manifest` re-fsyncs spine_dir) is correct; no bug.\n- **F5:** no legitimate Slice-0 state produces `active() is None` — `initial_manifest` always emits exactly one unsealed segment and nothing seals in Slice 0 — so the `raise` is correct fail-closed, not a store-unconstructable landmine.\n- **Headline (no-manifest path):** `_refresh_active_under_lock` and `_read_target` are true no-ops when no manifest exists (both resolve to `self.path`); record content is byte-identical (verified). The only deviation is Finding 2's lockfile.\n\n**Riskiest thing I cleared:** F3's index-offset validity across a migrate. The index is built over the pre-migrate file but `get()`/`_ensure_index` re-resolve the file per call, so a same-call migrate could read bytes from a *different* file than the one it sized. I confirmed this can **not** silently corrupt `_scan_pos`/offsets — Slice-0 migrate is a pure byte-preserving rename and the active file only ever grows, so any offset computed over the prefix stays valid on the resolved file. The *only* failure mode of that multi-resolution pattern is Finding 1's `FileNotFoundError`, not a silent wrong read. That guarantee is Slice-0-specific: it breaks the moment a later slice moves bytes (gzip-on-seal/compaction), so it must be re-verified there.\n\nRepro scripts are in the scratchpad (`repro.py`, `repro2c.py`, `repro_lock.py`) if you want to re-run them."}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":582,"cache_read_input_tokens":125111,"output_tokens":6535,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":582},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":6535,"cache_read_input_tokens":125111,"cache_creation_input_tokens":582,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":582,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdBjbBNtTbXCG2i6cnQBP","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"a9c48737-84cc-465b-b225-f49d6cf076c0","timestamp":"2026-07-19T16:32:28.622Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
